self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7fe0e82cd100>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def _add_coords(self, da: sc.DataArray, coords: sc.DataGroup) -> sc.DataArray:
        """Add coords to a data array.
    
        Sets alignment in the same way as slicing scipp.DataArray would.
        """
        for name, coord in coords.items():
            if not isinstance(coord, sc.Variable):
                da.coords[name] = sc.scalar(coord)
            else:
                if coord.shape == (1,) and not set(coord.dims).issubset(da.dims):
                    da.coords[name] = coord[0]
                else:

          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.

    def _add_coords(self, da: sc.DataArray, coords: sc.DataGroup) -> sc.DataArray:
        """Add coords to a data array.
    
        Sets alignment in the same way as slicing scipp.DataArray would.

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f35302ef320>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f719c17f9e0>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def _add_coords(self, da: sc.DataArray, coords: sc.DataGroup) -> sc.DataArray:
        """Add coords to a data array.
    
        Sets alignment in the same way as slicing scipp.DataArray would.
        """
        for name, coord in coords.items():
            if not isinstance(coord, sc.Variable):
                da.coords[name] = sc.scalar(coord)
            else:
                if coord.shape == (1,) and not set(coord.dims).issubset(da.dims):
                    da.coords[name] = coord[0]
                else:

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7fc160358560>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def _add_coords(self, da: sc.DataArray, coords: sc.DataGroup) -> sc.DataArray:
        """Add coords to a data array.
    
        Sets alignment in the same way as slicing scipp.DataArray would.
        """
        for name, coord in coords.items():
            if not isinstance(coord, sc.Variable):
                da.coords[name] = sc.scalar(coord)
            else:
                if coord.shape == (1,) and not set(coord.dims).issubset(da.dims):
                    da.coords[name] = coord[0]
                else:

    def _add_coords(self, da: sc.DataArray, coords: sc.DataGroup) -> sc.DataArray:
        """Add coords to a data array.

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f1c195281d0>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f0d5c3e0a10>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def _add_coords(self, da: sc.DataArray, coords: sc.DataGroup) -> sc.DataArray:
        """Add coords to a data array.
    
        Sets alignment in the same way as slicing scipp.DataArray would.
        """
        for name, coord in coords.items():
            if not isinstance(coord, sc.Variable):
                da.coords[name] = sc.scalar(coord)
            else:
                if coord.shape == (1,) and not set(coord.dims).issubset(da.dims):

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7fa930376c00>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def _add_coords(self, da: sc.DataArray, coords: sc.DataGroup) -> sc.DataArray:
        """Add coords to a data array.
    
        Sets alignment in the same way as slicing scipp.DataArray would.
        """
        for name, coord in coords.items():
            if not isinstance(coord, sc.Variable):
                da.coords[name] = sc.scalar(coord)
            else:
                if coord.shape == (1,) and not set(coord.dims).issubset(da.dims):

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7ff804248260>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7fb4dc322c30>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def _add_coords(self, da: sc.DataArray, coords: sc.DataGroup) -> sc.DataArray:
        """Add coords to a data array.
    
        Sets alignment in the same way as slicing scipp.DataArray would.
        """
        for name, coord in coords.items():
            if not isinstance(coord, sc.Variable):
                da.coords[name] = sc.scalar(coord)
            else:
                if coord.shape == (1,) and not set(coord.dims).issubset(da.dims):
                    da.coords[name] = coord[0]
                else:

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f9eb01d0710>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def _add_coords(self, da: sc.DataArray, coords: sc.DataGroup) -> sc.DataArray:
        """Add coords to a data array.
    
        Sets alignment in the same way as slicing scipp.DataArray would.

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f8e0c2e4e90>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7fc09a7cc560>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def _add_coords(self, da: sc.DataArray, coords: sc.DataGroup) -> sc.DataArray:
        """Add coords to a data array.
    
        Sets alignment in the same way as slicing scipp.DataArray would.
        """
        for name, coord in coords.items():
            if not isinstance(coord, sc.Variable):
                da.coords[name] = sc.scalar(coord)
            else:
                if coord.shape == (1,) and not set(coord.dims).issubset(da.dims):
                    da.coords[name] = coord[0]
                else:

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7fb6bbeaf920>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def _add_coords(self, da: sc.DataArray, coords: sc.DataGroup) -> sc.DataArray:
        """Add coords to a data array.
    
        Sets alignment in the same way as slicing scipp.DataArray would.
        """
        for name, coord in coords.items():
            if not isinstance(coord, sc.Variable):
                da.coords[name] = sc.scalar(coord)
            else:
                if coord.shape == (1,) and not set(coord.dims).issubset(da.dims):
                    da.coords[name] = coord[0]
                else:

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7fef5bec6090>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f059b787350>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def _add_coords(self, da: sc.DataArray, coords: sc.DataGroup) -> sc.DataArray:
        """Add coords to a data array.
    
        Sets alignment in the same way as slicing scipp.DataArray would.
        """
        for name, coord in coords.items():
            if not isinstance(coord, sc.Variable):
                da.coords[name] = sc.scalar(coord)
            else:
                if coord.shape == (1,) and not set(coord.dims).issubset(da.dims):
                    da.coords[name] = coord[0]
                else:

          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def _add_coords(self, da: sc.DataArray, coords: sc.DataGroup) -> sc.DataArray:
        """Add coords to a data array.

        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f4e3ee562a0>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

self = <scippnexus.base.Group object at 0x7f575ee31b50>, sel = ()

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def _add_coords(self, da: sc.DataArray, coords: sc.DataGroup) -> sc.DataArray:
        """Add coords to a data array.
    
        Sets alignment in the same way as slicing scipp.DataArray would.
        """
        for name, coord in coords.items():
            if not isinstance(coord, sc.Variable):
                da.coords[name] = sc.scalar(coord)
            else:
                if coord.shape == (1,) and not set(coord.dims).issubset(da.dims):
                    da.coords[name] = coord[0]
                else:

            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """
        if isinstance(sel, str):
            # We cannot get the child directly from the HDF5 group, since we need to
            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
            # such as sizes and dtype.
            if '/' in sel:
                sel_path = PurePosixPath(sel)
                if sel_path.is_absolute():
                    return self.file[sel_path.relative_to('/').as_posix()]
                # If the path is a single name, we can directly access the child
                elif len(sel_path.parts) == 1:
                    return self[sel_path.as_posix()]
                else:
                    grp = sel_path.parts[0]
                    return self[grp][sel_path.relative_to(grp).as_posix()]
            elif sel == '..':
                return self.parent
            child = self._children[sel]
            if isinstance(child, Field):
                self._populate_fields()
            return child
    
        def isclass(x):
            return inspect.isclass(x) and issubclass(x, Field | NXobject)
    
        if isclass(sel) or (
            isinstance(sel, list) and len(sel) and all(isclass(x) for x in sel)
        ):
            return self._get_children_by_nx_class(sel)
    
        dg = self._nexus.read_children(sel)
        if not dg:
            # Bail out early to avoid fallback warnings. Everything is optional in
            # NeXus so we cannot assume that the group is invalid (in contrast to
            # likely partially incomplete groups that will fail to assemble below).
            return dg
        try:
>           dg = self._nexus.assemble(dg)
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def _add_coords(self, da: sc.DataArray, coords: sc.DataGroup) -> sc.DataArray:
        """Add coords to a data array.

    def __getitem__(self, sel):
        """
        Get a child group or child dataset, a selection of child groups, or load and
        return the current group.
    
        Three cases are supported:
    
        - String name: The child group or child dataset of that name is returned.
        - Class such as ``NXdata`` or ``NXlog``: A dict containing all direct children
          with a matching ``NX_class`` attribute are returned. Also accepts a tuple of
          classes. ``Field`` selects all child fields, i.e., all datasets but not
          groups.
        - Scipp-style index: Load the specified slice of the current group, returning
          a :class:`scipp.DataArray` or :class:`scipp.DataGroup`.
    
        Parameters
        ----------
        sel:
            Child name, class, or index.
    
        Returns
        -------
        :
            Field, group, dict of fields, or loaded data.
        """

            # create the parent group, to ensure that fields get the correct properties
